| TASK-WINDOWS-UX-001 | AGENT-WINDOWS-UX (taskbar identity lane, 2026-09-24) | P1 | Windows taskbar application identity = NexusTraderBot (was "python"). ROOT CAUSE: NSE is a console app whose running process is python.exe/pythonw.exe (source) or the PyInstaller bootloader (packaged); Windows derives the taskbar label/icon/grouping from the process AppUserModelID, and NSE never set one, so the shell fell back to the hosting image name. A window title alone cannot fix the taskbar label because the entry is keyed on the AppUserModelID, not on SetWindowText. FIX (layered, one canonical owner): (1) NEW src/nexus_scalp/platform/windows_identity.py - TASKBAR_APP_ID="Opselon.NexusTraderBot" (stable/deterministic/unique) + TASKBAR_APP_NAME="NexusTraderBot" + apply_windows_identity() calling SetCurrentProcessExplicitAppUserModelID + SetConsoleTitleW, guarded `sys.platform=="win32"`, ctypes imported inside the guarded body only (import-safe on Linux/macOS CI), failure-isolated (branding can never block engine boot), idempotent (restarts group into ONE taskbar entry - no ghost "python" group); (2) invoked at the FIRST line of every entrypoint BEFORE any render/import of the CLI tree: packaged_main.py + cli_shim.py (frozen EXE), NexusTradingForexBot.py (source launcher) and _run_engine_locked in cli/engine_boot.py (nexus start/daemon); engine_boot ordering verified identity-before-anchor_workspace; (3) NEW scripts/build/generate_version_info.py + --version-file on BOTH PyInstaller builds (build_release.ps1 + release.yml onedir AND onefile) embed PE VSVersionInfo ProductName/FileDescription/InternalName=NexusTraderBot, CompanyName=Opselon, OriginalFilename=NexusScalpEngine.exe; (4) installer/NexusScalpEngine.iss MyAppName/MyAppPublisher + Start Menu/desktop shortcut captions = NexusTraderBot. VERIFIED ON WINDOWS (not just source-read): source launcher child process AUMID read back = Opselon.NexusTraderBot + console title = NexusTraderBot; a REAL PyInstaller --onefile build of the same import path reports FROZEN=True APPLIED=True AUMID=Opselon.NexusTraderBot CONSOLE_TITLE=NexusTraderBot; PE version-info parsed out of a REAL built EXE: ProductName='NexusTraderBot', FileDescription='NexusTraderBot', InternalName='NexusTraderBot', CompanyName='Opselon', OriginalFilename='NexusScalpEngine.exe', FileVersion/ProductVersion='9.0.14.0'. 19 new tests + 2 platform skips; ruff check+format clean repo-wide; mypy src/nexus_scalp/platform clean (the one residual mypy error, official_install.py os.O_DIRECTORY, is pre-existing at origin/main - a Linux-only flag on Windows); 2 test_packaged_db_and_mode_bug146_149 failures proven pre-existing at origin/main (missing model artifact on this box). HONEST LIMITATION: dev/source launch still runs python.exe - the taskbar label becomes NexusTraderBot (AUMID + console title), but Task Manager's process *image name* column remains python.exe by OS design; only the packaged NexusScalpEngine.exe is branded end-to-end at the image level. | none | src/nexus_scalp/platform/windows_identity.py (new), src/nexus_scalp/platform/__init__.py (new), src/nexus_scalp/release/packaged_main.py, src/nexus_scalp/release/cli_shim.py, src/nexus_scalp/cli/engine_boot.py, NexusTradingForexBot.py, scripts/build/generate_version_info.py (new), scripts/build/build_release.ps1, .github/workflows/release.yml, installer/NexusScalpEngine.iss, .gitignore, tests/unit/test_windows_taskbar_identity.py (new), agents/taskboard.md (this row) | Windows AppUserModelID/taskbar grouping contract, packaged EXE entrypoint import order, installer shortcut captions, PE version-info fields | none | IN_PROGRESS (PR pending) |

| TASK-NEWS-ADMISSION | Hermes (news-admission-gate lane, 2026-09-23) | P1 | Pre-DB News Admission Gateway: NewsAdmissionGateway (STAGE 0-4 progressive-cost gates composing the EXISTING LocalNewsAnalyzer + calendar.classify_event_title + source tier weights) wired into NewsIngestor BEFORE insert_article. Verdict = ADMIT/QUARANTINE/REJECT with score + machine-readable reason codes; REJECT tombstones via the recoverable junk-hash path (same mechanism as auto-prune), QUARANTINE persists with article_status=QUARANTINE and is excluded from list_articles (never reaches analysis/features). STAGE 2b exact (source_id, canonical_url) guard closes the dominant duplicate vector: read-only audit found 21,771 of 24,588 rows (88.5%) are re-ingests under a repeated (source,url) - one Bank of England notice stored 231x - because fabricated per-poll published_at re-mints the BUG-282 time-bucketed article_hash; same-URL with a CHANGED summary still creates a version row (test_12 contract preserved). Config centralized as NewsAdmissionThresholds on NewsConfig. EVIDENCE: scripts/audit/news_dataset_audit.py + scripts/benchmarks/benchmark_news_admission_50k.py - 5,000-item workload: 98.76% DB writes avoided vs 95.96% baseline (62 vs 202 persisted); HONEST CAVEAT in PR: end-to-end throughput is currently LOWER with the gateway (16.7 vs 34.9 items/s) because the guard adds an indexed SELECT per surviving item and per-item SQLite commits dominate - the win is write-avoidance + junk-hash short-circuit, not raw speed. 21 new admission tests; ruff/format/mypy clean; 176 passed incl. full phase12 + critical news files; 11 test_news_api failures proven pre-existing on clean origin/main. | none | src/nexus_scalp/news/admission.py (new), ingest/fetcher.py, db_articles.py, db_schema.py, config.py, engine.py, tests/unit/test_news_admission_gate.py (new), scripts/audit/news_dataset_audit.py, scripts/benchmarks/benchmark_news_admission_50k.py, agents/taskboard.md (this row) | news ingest funnel, auto_prune floors (importance<0.30 / xauusd<0.25), BUG-282 hash identity, test_12 versioning contract | none | MERGED (PR #410, commit 5c38c3ef) |

| TASK-ML-POSITION-FORENSICS | Hermes (ML-position-forensics lane, 2026-09-24) | CRITICAL | Forensic audit + production hardening of the ENTIRE ML Position Management lifecycle (position_adviser train/serve, dataset generation/replay, order-manager coupling). PROVEN DEFECTS fixed and regression-pinned: F0 CRITICAL — integration.py derived distance_to_target_r/distance_to_stop_r in the WRONG units (dollar distance / price-unit divisor instead of the generator's price/risk-amount convention), so every live advisory was served features bearing no relation to its own labels; also holding_duration was out of scope in _evaluate_hold_score (now an explicit holding_duration_sec parameter). F1 HIGH — no staleness gate, no duplicate-decision gate (a replayed/delayed tick could re-decide the same state), unbounded per-ticket maps; now max_snapshot_age_sec + per-ticket snapshot_id duplicate gate BEFORE throttle commit, _CAP-bounded maps, adviser.forget() wired at broker-verified close, stale_rejected_count + decision-trace diagnostics. F2 HIGH — the replay split boundary was decorative: OOS label horizons crossing the val/oos boundary were not quarantined and causality_violations was hardcoded 0; now tail-truncation + embargo at every boundary and a REAL train<val<oos ordering check. F6 CRITICAL — service.load never read the sidecar manifest, so a model from dataset A could load with dataset B's scaler/schema; now one canonical verify_model_package gate (weights+scaler sha256, feature_order/feature_dim, dataset hash) shared by the service and the new `position-adviser-packages` CLI. F7 MEDIUM — training was not reproducible (identical seed -> byte-different artifacts), which defeats the F6 hash pin; now seeded + deterministic flags BEFORE the loop (contract is on the weights, which verify numerically identical — torch.save archives differ by basename by design). F8 MEDIUM — an absent training class got weight 1/max(freq,1e-9)=1e9, collapsing all observed class weights and driving cross-entropy to a loss in the MILLIONS with no error; now zero weight + reported by name in classes_absent. | TASK-ML-LABEL-002 | AGENT-LABEL (2026-09-18) | P1 | Sample Uniqueness Weighting & Label Overlap Anti-Leakage: Implemented Marcos Lopez de Prado's Sample Uniqueness Weighting algorithm (AFML Ch. 4). Vectorized O(M + N) difference array + prefix sum concurrency engine computes concurrent events c_t and average uniqueness u_i in (0, 1] in 13.4ms on 50,000 rows (<2.0s SLA). Strict non-overlapping samples yield u_i = 1.0; identical overlaps yield u_i = 1/K; partial overlaps yield mathematically exact fractional uniqueness. Integrated with Polars DataFrames (handling unevaluated sample masks), sample weight normalization (sum to N), return attribution (w = u * |r|), exponential time-decay, SampleUniquenessReport (Kish effective sample size N_eff, redundancy ratio), Parquet artifact export with companion .meta.json SHA-256 manifests, and PyTorch training integration (WeightedTensorDataset, create_weighted_dataloader with standard and WeightedRandomSampler modes, SampleWeightedCrossEntropyLoss, SampleWeightedFocalLoss). 22 automated unit tests (tests/unit/test_sample_weights.py) registered in tests/critical_suite.txt. Empirical study across 10,000 Gold M1 bars (mean u_i = 0.8988, N_eff = 2,765.2, max c_t = 4) documented in docs/research/SAMPLE_UNIQUENESS_EMPIRICAL_STUDY.md. | ML-LABEL-001 | src/nexus_scalp/labeling/sample_weights.py, src/nexus_scalp/labeling/__init__.py, scripts/analysis/analyze_sample_uniqueness.py, docs/research/SAMPLE_UNIQUENESS_EMPIRICAL_STUDY.md, tests/unit/test_sample_weights.py, tests/critical_suite.txt | sample uniqueness weighting, AFML Ch. 4, IID compliance, zero lookahead | none | DONE |
| TASK-BUG270-HEADERSPLIT | Hermes (code-scanning lane, 2026-09-13) | P1 (security, medium sev) | CLOSED the 9 open CodeQL py/http-response-splitting alerts (#1086-#1094) on scripts/serve_alt_ui.py — all in the STANDALONE-ALT-UI host's echo/relay header sinks. ROOT CAUSE (probe-verified on CPython 3.11): the stdlib email parser keeps obs-fold CR/LF inside parsed header values and BaseHTTPRequestHandler.send_header writes its buffer with zero validation, so echoed request-ids and relayed upstream names/values could split the response. FIX (stdlib-only, layered): request-id charset allowlist at _dispatch; RFC-token allowlist on relayed upstream NAMES; header_safe() CR/LF strip at every relay/echo sink; send_header() override as handler-wide choke point; HEAD content-length echoes only digits. Validators are LINEAR set/length checks, not regexes (PR #184 push-1 drew a NEW py/polynomial-redos HIGH alert on the tchar regex fed upstream bytes; push-2 replaced both regexes — the lesson: a security fix must not itself scan attacker bytes with a regex). VERIFICATION (no guessing): CodeQL CLI 2.27.0 (same version as the alerts) + the exact codeql/python-queries HeaderInjection.ql AND PolynomialReDoS.ql run LOCALLY — pre-fix file reproduced ALL 9 alerts at the alert lines, final file = 0 alerts on both. Tests: +8 pins TestHeaderInjectionDefense (RED 6/7 pre-fix), suite 49/49 green; ruff check+format repo-wide clean; mypy clean. FINDINGS (docs/agent_handoffs/2026-09-09_NSE_storage_hygiene_findings.txt): (A2) log retention ran ONLY at boot — _prune_old_logs had exactly one caller (configure_logging); long-lived clients accumulated every post-boot severity part file. FIXED: maybe_prune_on_emit() day-roll hook re-arms the hourly prune inside a running process (only the INFO-severity handler triggers; fault-swallowed per BUG-122). (A3/A4) added per-severity byte budget (DEFAULT_MAX_MB_PER_SEVERITY=500, oldest-first, active file protected) + gzip compression of logs older than DEFAULT_COMPRESS_AFTER_DAYS=2 (verify-before-unlink). (C2/C3) update cache NEVER pruned (update_home/cache accumulates every release package) + .previous-* full-app backups accumulate (pointer cleanup was an explicit no-op) — FIXED via prune_update_cache(keep=2), sweep_crash_leftovers(keep_previous_backups=1; freshness = newest FILE in tree, dir mtime unreliable), residue sweep (stale *.tmp/*.part >1h, depth-bounded). (B3) hygiene spec promised WAL/VACUUM-in-maint-window but zero implementations existed — StorageGuard._wal_checkpoint_pass runs PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE) on the 3 managed DBs (SQLite-magic-header validated first: blind connect on a foreign file can make sqlite3 DELETE the sidecar); auto-VACUUM refused by design. (C9) quota_snapshot() measures logs/cache/backups/diagnostics/databases vs storage.quota_mb (alert-only). (D1) MT5 diagnostics failure STORM: every failed call logged an identical unthrottled WARNING (retry cadence ~100ms = measured multi-GB log driver) — signature-keyed 30s suppression window, first failure always logs, repeats demoted to DEBUG with counter, honest FAILURE_STORM_SUMMARY on window close, recoveries never suppressed. New `src/nexus_scalp/training/optimizers.py`: `build_optimizer_and_scheduler(model, config, *, epochs, steps_per_epoch)` covering 4 optimizers (adam / adamw / sgd / lookahead — the latter a stdlib+torch implementation of Zhang et al. 2019, no third-party dep per NON_GOALS) and 6 schedulers (none / cosine / cosine_restarts / plateau / one_cycle / linear_decay) with linear warmup composing over any of them. The factory removes four raw-torch traps at every call site: (1) step CADENCE — the returned `scheduler_step_every_batch` flag tells the caller whether to step per batch (one_cycle / linear_decay / batch-cadence cosine_restarts) or per epoch (cosine / plateau); both trainers now honor it; (2) ReduceLROnPlateau requires the metric while every other scheduler rejects it — `step_scheduler` routes the argument only where valid and raises OptimizerConfigError otherwise; (3) the `lr_scheduler.step() before optimizer.step()` warning is asserted absent across the full matrix under `warnings.simplefilter("error")`; (4) `Adam(weight_decay=...)` is L2 not decoupled, so adam + positive decay silently routes to AdamW (pinned by test). Integrated into CandidateTrainer (reads `experiment.training["optimizer_config"]`, records optimizer+scheduler on the manifest) and WalkForwardTrainer (opt-in `optimizer_config=`, default preserves the exact historical AdamW 5e-4/wd 1e-4 + CosineAnnealingLR recipe and now records it in `last_convergence_metadata["optimizer_recipe"]`). Audit published to `docs/research/OPTIMIZER_SCHEDULER_AUDIT.md` with the constant-vs-cosine benchmark table and an honest reading: on the 96-row synthetic probe a well-tuned constant LR wins, and cosine's advantage appears as safety at a WRONG (too low) LR — deliberately not generalized to a production verdict since a synthetic run cannot measure OOS generalization on months of M1 bars (that is a walk-forward A/B, ML-EXP territory). | ML-TRAIN-002 | AGENT-ML-TRAIN (swarm cycle 2026-09-21) | P2 | Loss Function Exploration: Focal / Label Smoothing / Class-Balanced / Dice — DONE. New `src/nexus_scalp/training/losses.py`: six losses behind one shared `forward(logits, targets, sample_weights=None)` signature plus a `build_loss(name, **kw)` factory (cross_entropy, focal, label_smoothing, focal_smoothing, class_balanced, dice) and `effective_number_weights`. Corrected the task's stale CURRENT_EVIDENCE: the candidate trainer already used FocalLossWithSmoothing (model_generation/training.py:270, class inline at walk_forward_trainer.py:2683); the real gap was no canonical config-selectable loss, so the five other hard-coded nn.CrossEntropyLoss sites could not be compared or swapped. FIVE bugs found by the test battery, all now regression-pinned: (1) in-place clamp_ on p_t broke autograd (out-of-place now); (2) ignore_index targets crashed the gather (safe_targets); (3) smooth_ce built inside torch.no_grad() killed EVERY gradient in both smoothing losses — loss computed fine, training learned nothing; (4) nn.CrossEntropyLoss(weight=) was double-weighted through _reduce; (5) the benchmark itself was vacuous — build_loss('label_smoothing') defaults to smoothing=0.0 which is bit-identical to CE, so that leg compared a loss against itself. Benchmark (5 identical-split folds, 384 samples, 80/11/9): focal +0.107 minority F1 over CE (0.8212 vs 0.7139), wins 4/5 folds, biggest gains on the worst folds. Label smoothing lowered the confidence ceiling but RAISED Brier (0.1056->0.1073) — reported honestly as a measured non-win: this synthetic model is under-confident, so Brier only moves the right way if the base model was over-confident; the guaranteed invariant (target capped below 1.0) is asserted directly instead. NON_GOALS honored: NO production loss changed; legacy FocalLossWithSmoothing left in place and asserted bit-identical; the five CE sites are follow-up work. TWO real bugs caught by the tests: (1) SQLite json_extract — the $['key'] bracket form resolves ARRAY indices not object keys on SQLite 3.53, silently NULLing every best/top-n query; json_path() now emits $.key and a test resolves the path through the real engine; (2) transaction rollback double-fire (ROLLBACK inside except re-raised through a closed transaction, 'cannot rollback - no transaction is active') — replaced with a _transaction() context manager that commits only on clean exit. | ML-ARCH-002 | AGENT-ML-ARCH (swarm cycle 2026-09-22) | P2 | Causal TCN Dilation & Receptive Field Optimization — DONE. Parameterized the dilation schedule in `CausalConv1dBlock`/`TCNAttentionV1` (geometric default unchanged; new linear + fibonacci) with exact receptive-field helpers (`dilation_schedule`, `receptive_field`, `min_blocks_for_receptive_field`) and `.dilations` / `.receptive_field` model properties; `build_tcn_attention_v1` forwards `dilation_schedule` from the ModelFactory params dict. AC-1 (causal invariance proven, not assumed): autograd Jacobian test asserts dY_t/dX_{t+k} == 0 exactly for every schedule x blocks x kernel-size combination (27 stack configs, 9 model conv-stage configs, 21 seq_len x schedule combos) in float64, plus two non-autograd cross-checks (a past perturbation moves the output; a future perturbation leaves strictly-earlier outputs bit-identical at atol=0). AC-2: closed form 1 + sum((k-1)*d_b) matches the realized model property for all 27 combinations AND an independent empirical impulse probe (per-channel LN-surviving perturbation, response measured at the anchor position) equals the closed form exactly. KEY FINDING (documented, not acted on): `CausalConv1dBlock`'s UNPADDED residual skip makes the block/stack receptive field the FULL SEQUENCE — the schedule controls only the conv-branch memory depth. Formula scope corrected accordingly and the report states plainly that the [16,32,64]-bar targets are conv-branch depth budgets, not end-to-end memory budgets. Backwards compatibility is exact, not approximate: the geometric default reproduces pre-ML-ARCH-002 wiring bit-for-bit (conv dilations, all parameters via torch.equal, forward output atol=0 rtol=0) so existing checkpoints are unaffected. Latency benchmark: bounded across RF depths and far below the 50ms budget for every schedule. Abort condition never fired. | ML-ARCH-003 | AGENT-ML-ARCH (swarm cycle 2026-09-22) | P2 | Temporal Multihead Attention vs Positional Encoding Ablation — DONE. New `src/nexus_scalp/models/attention.py` (CausalSelfAttention with the triangular causal mask as a CONSTRUCTION property not a caller obligation — the exact bug the task names at architectures.py:273 and scalp_net.py:231; make_causal_mask always returns an additive float mask, never bool, because a bool attn_mask inverts the convention and would silently permit the future taps this module exists to prevent; rotate_half/apply_rotary RoPE primitives; none/sinusoidal/learned/rotary encoders behind one POSITIONAL_ENCODINGS registry) and `src/nexus_scalp/models/attention_ablation.py` (AttentionArm spec, NoAttentionArm pure-TCN control, AttentionAttentionArm, build_attention_ablation_arms over attention x heads x positional with the heads dimension collapsed for the no-attention control so no phantom cells are reported, run_attention_ablation with a SHARED batch-order RNG stream so every arm sees identical minibatches, compute_macro_f1 sklearn-free following sklearn's macro convention where a class absent from both preds and labels is SKIPPED not zero-counted, compute_val_metrics restoring caller train/eval mode, measure_latency on time.process_time median for co-tenant-runner insensitivity). AC-1 (strict causal masking) proven three ways: autograd Jacobian dY_t/dX_{t+k} == 0 exactly in float64 for heads 2/4 and seq lens 7/16; returned attention weights carry zero mass above the diagonal; empirical past-perturbation probe leaves earlier outputs bit-identical. The incumbent unmasked layer is proven NON-causal by the same Jacobian method (future block > 0). AC-2: docs/research/ATTENTION_ABLATION.md tabulates the full 20-arm matrix — attention improves val loss 0.283->0.225 (-20%) and accuracy +0.5pt for a latency cost of +187%..+320% (558..955 us/sample) against a sub-ms live SLA; the causal mask alone costs +396 us/sample and buys NOTHING on the decision. KEY FINDING (proven, not assumed): the causal mask is a NO-OP at the decision row of a last-timestep-pooled model — causal and unmasked arms return bit-identical loss/accuracy across all 8 matched pairs (max diff 0.0) because position T-1's allowed set (0..T-1) equals its full allowed set without a mask. The mask is a correctness invariant for INTERMEDIATE sequence states, not a predictive-loss knob; an ablation that flips only the mask while pooling the last timestep is degenerate by construction. Reported to the operator: do not pay the mask latency premium on a pooled model, and apply the mask only if mid-window states are ever consumed. NON_GOALS honored: no live-serving path modified (TCNAttentionV1, ScalpNet, model_factory.py, model_lab/architectures.py all untouched); research/ablation components only. Abort conditions never fired. | ML-CI-002 | AGENT-GIT (swarm cycle 2026-09-21) | P3 | ML Contract & Canonical Documentation Drift Prevention CI Gate — DONE. New scripts/ci/check_ml_contract_drift.py: parses 8 canonical ML constants from source via anchored regex (ACTIVE_SCHEMA_ID, FEATURE_DIMENSION, TRAINED_CLASS_COUNT, TRAINED_CLASS_NAMES, LEGACY_HEAD_CLASSES, WAIT_LOGIT_INDEX, SCALP_V3_DIMENSION, HARD_MAX_LOTS) and asserts each is stated with a matching value in the four canonical docs (01_SYSTEM_CONTRACT / 02_DATA_CONTRACT / 03_MODEL_ARCHITECTURE / 05_INFERENCE_FORWARDTEST_GOVERNANCE). Two finding classes: STALE_DOC (a doc line asserts a value that differs from source — only assignment/copula shapes count, so historical prose like '3 trained, 1 dead logit' never fires) and MISSING_DOC (the declaration moved/renamed, or the docs never cited the constant). Direction of truth is SOURCE per 01 §2; regex-on-source not import so the static CI lane pulls no torch/polars. Wired BOTH sides by design: .github/workflows/ci.yml ci-integrity lane (new step) AND scripts/ci/check_local.py stage [5b] ml_contract_drift — no CI-only gate class, which is exactly what scripts/ci/gate_parity.py exists to catch (6/6 PASS, 0 drifts). Real coverage gap found and fixed rather than excepted: docs/ml-system never cited the position-size ceiling anywhere, so §2.2 'Execution Ceiling (Position Safety Contract)' was added to 05 citing HARD_MAX_LOTS = 10.0 + MAX_TOTAL_EXPOSURE = 1. ## GOV-OWNERSHIP-001 — branch/worktree ownership hardening (2026-09-22)

| TASK-ID | Owner | Priority | Title | Deps | Files | Contracts | Blocker | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| TASK-GOV-OWNERSHIP-001 | Hermes (governance lane, 2026-09-22) | P1 | Branch/worktree ownership safety after the 2026-09-22 incident: a concurrent agent committed to `main` while the primary worktree was mid-synchronisation, because nothing asserted who owns a branch before a mutation. Adds `check_branch_ownership` + `check_task_identity` to `scripts/git/preflight.py` (BRANCH_OWNED_BY_OTHER_WORKTREE / FOREIGN_BRANCH / BRANCH_MOVED_UNDER_US are critical STOP conditions; detached HEAD warns about unattributable work), new `agents/git_governance.md` §8a (ownership resolution before switch/checkout/commit/merge/rebase/reset/update-ref/branch -f/worktree add-remove/cherry-pick/revert/push; ownership can change mid-task; resolve against the inspected repo, never the process cwd), 10 fixture-only regression tests for the race class, `beforePush.ps1` divergence WARN-only -> STOP plus delegation to the canonical preflight, `nightly-qa.yml` chaos failure now fails the job instead of only annotating, DEC-0006 -> DEC-0008(d) supersession chain made explicit, `git-release-guardian.md` §9 direct-merge-permission removed, `multi-agent-git-contract.md` §16 branch-naming conflict reconciled (canonical `agent/<type>/<ID>` from `agents/git_governance.md` §5; legacy `agent/<name>/<task>` superseded not forbidden), and `docs/engineering/release-process.md` version literal removed in favour of the pyproject canonical source. | GOV-GIT-001 (PR #352) | scripts/git/preflight.py, tests/unit/test_git_governance.py, agents/git_governance.md, agents/multi-agent-git-contract.md, agents/git-release-guardian.md, agents/decisions/DEC-0006-branch-protection-docs-required-context.md, beforePush.ps1, .github/workflows/nightly-qa.yml, docs/engineering/release-process.md, agents/taskboard.md | no production code changed; preflight is read-only | none | PR pending |

### Incident root cause (evidence, not inference)

Concurrent-agent activity on the feature branch while the primary worktree was
mid-synchronisation: reflog `agent/feature/ML-PHASE1-DATA-CONTRACT` shows a
reset to the freshly-merged main (`2d3eb77b`, 23:17:25), then UI commits
(23:29:00) and two cherry-picks (23:34:09, 23:35:02) including the commit that
briefly appeared on `main` (`34e0b052`, 23:19:37). That commit's pro-panel
content was later re-cherry-picked onto `agent/hermes/frontend-pro-uiux` and
evolved further, landing on `main` via PR #355 (`db781306`). The intermediate
`34e0b052` itself is preserved on `backup/main-34e0b052-neural-studio-pro-panel`
(rebased as `209caa45`) and is NOT part of this governance PR.

## UI-PERF-007 — runtime perf wave (2026-09-23)
| TASK-ID | Owner | Priority | Title | Deps | Files | Contracts | Blocker | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TASK-UI-PERF-007 | Hermes (ui-perf wave-7 lane, 2026-09-23) | P1 | Pro UI/UX PERFORMANCE wave 7 (user brief: "next wave strong work on ui ux performance speed") — three measured runtime fixes on the operator hot path. The default Dashboard route re-renders on every backend SSE telemetry frame (5 Hz, web/server.py `await asyncio.sleep(0.2)`), and each render did ~40 format* calls plus a full active-route REMOUNT. (1) lib/format.ts — memoized Intl.NumberFormat/DateTimeFormat keyed on the option tuple; measured Node 24 10k calls toLocaleString 640ms vs cached 47ms = 13.6x; locale strings pinned to en-US/en-GB/en-CA so output is BYTE-IDENTICAL (pinned by 16 assertions vs the exact previous strings); blank/NaN short-circuit before ICU; incidental fix formatMoney(-0) was emitting the malformed "$ -0.00", now "$0.00" (pinned). (2) core/realtime.ts — coalesced listener notification via queueMicrotask, latest snapshot wins; correct because the state_version out-of-order guard already guarantees monotonicity within a stream; coreEvents.publish + status listeners stay immediate (liveness). (3) app/AppShell.tsx — the 20 feature <Route element={...}> were built INLINE in JSX so every render allocated fresh element objects; React Router reconciles by element identity so the ACTIVE route REMOUNTED on every tick (unmount, DOM destroy, effect re-run incl. chart init) 5x/sec 24/7; useMemo keyed [snapshot, nowMs, routePathname] keeps identity stable while still re-rendering the page when data changes (re-render != remount). VERIFIED: tests/js/perf_wave7_format.test.js 16/16 under the exact CI invocation (node <file>, bare — the .js extension is REQUIRED because js-tests.yml only globs tests/js/*.test.js, .mjs is excluded from the gate); full JS gate 16 .test.js files green; tsc -b --noEmit clean; npm run build EXIT=0; entry chunk 49.9->50.2KB (no bloat); both fixes located in the served bundle (realtime-Dm0MI4RK.js queueMicrotask, format-Bt5UV6lB.js cached Intl). FALSE POSITIVE recorded: `nexus repair --json` stdout is 100% pure JSON over a real subprocess — the apparent pollution was typer CliRunner merging stderr into stdout (CliRunner cannot prove a JSON-purity contract). VERIFIED green: doctor/health/status/config/logs/update-check/model-provision/verify-release/uninstall-help all correct with valid JSON and correct RC codes, all CWD-independent. ### Documented gaps NOT fixed here (evidence in docs/audit/eu_release_findings.md; each needs its own lane)
- G1 dual user-data roots — Inno/app use %LOCALAPPDATA%/NexusScalpEngine (config nexus.yaml); the PowerShell installer uses %LOCALAPPDATA%/Nexus/config (base.yaml / live.yaml), so its provisioned templates are never read by the app, and the documented NEXUS_HOME override is read by nothing in src/. Fixing this edits release/paths.py (every health/repair/diagnostic resolves through it) — out of scope for a packaging lane.
- G2 EXE version metadata — no PyInstaller --version-file, so the shell property sheet lacks FileVersion/ProductVersion (runtime `nexus version` identity IS correct via stamped build-info.json). Deferred: a malformed version file would RED the release workflow; needs a real ISCC/pyinstaller validation run.
- G3 dead first-run marker — Inno writes config/.first_run ("the CLI reads this to know the wizard should run") but no src/ code reads it; the wizard still launches via the [Run] post-install step, so user behavior is correct and the marker is dead weight.

## LOG-DEFECTS-WAVE — live log defect remediation (2026-09-23)
| TASK-ID | Owner | Priority | Title | Deps | Files | Contracts | Blocker | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| TASK-LOG-DEFECTS-001 | Hermes (multi-agent lane wave nse-logdefects, 2026-09-23) | P0 | Four real defects behind the live engine log noise, executed under a frozen 4-lane contract (log-defects-wave/CONTRACT.md) with 4 isolated worktrees on base 6a8aa2cb (= origin/main). LD-1 [FORENSIC_ALERT check=CHECK-ACC-02 status=CRITICAL every ~10s] was a FALSE POSITIVE: 1,465 of 1,687 audit_experience_outcomes rows are non-executed paper samples with execution_id='' and is_executed=0 (verified read-only against live artifacts/audit.db); the check grouped them as one 'execution identity with >1 outcome'. Only 2 genuine duplicate groups exist (152494870397, 152660983978 — both immutable pre-BUG-097-guard rows). Fix: empty/absent execution identity is excluded from the duplicate scan (counted as evidence, never whitelisted as an allowed duplicate — a genuine future duplicate on an empty id is still surfaced), 152660983978 joined the known-historical set, CRITICAL is preserved for NEW non-empty duplicates. Functional probe against the live DB: CRITICAL -> WARNING (both real dupes still reported). LD-2 [58 'Duplicate Operation ID' warnings per app build]: root cause is NOT a double registration — register_intelligence_routes/register_model_governance_routes only DECORATE the module-level APIRouter (@router.*), and app.include_router is the SOLE mount; FastAPI's include_router is non-idempotent and the module router is process-global state, so the 2nd+ create_app() in a process re-mounted the accumulated router (the forensic sweep did that every 7-20s — LD-3). Fix: clear the module router immediately before each register (exactly-once per app, original route ORDER preserved). The original CONTRACT.md instructed deleting the include_router calls — that would have UNREGISTERED all 55 /api/intelligence/* + /api/models/* routes; lane B read the producer and rejected it, and the contract was corrected in-commit for provenance. LD-3 [the [ALT-UI] serving line + the full warning dump repeating every 7-20s] root cause: checks_observability.check_api_200_but_wrong() called create_app() + app.openapi() on EVERY forensic sweep to test set membership of 5 paths. Fix: resolve the path surface once per process, keyed by the create_app callable (not a bare global set — that is poisoned by an earlier build and blinds a test that swaps the factory), recomputed from the cached app if its route count changed, failures never cached (stays retriable + fail-closed UNKNOWN). Probe: create_app called 1x over 5 sweeps, verdict PASS. LD-4 [WORKER_KICK TIMEOUT worker=RESEARCH every 60-90s]: the worker is NOT hung — its own UPDATE lines show duration_ms 124953/132346/149451 with work_done=False, so every healthy 125-150s cycle was abandoned by the 45s kick budget and re-run. Default NSE_WORKER_KICK_TIMEOUT raised 45 -> 180 (env override still wins; documented why so it is not lowered back), and the TIMEOUT line now carries a best-effort last_cycle_duration hint (worker.__self__ -> last_cycle_duration / cycle_duration_ms) so an operator can tell slow-but-healthy from wedged; the detach mechanism itself is unchanged. LD-5 [NEWS_FETCH forexlive WinError 10061] investigated and classified NO-CODE: live news.db news_health shows forexlive consecutive_failures=6 healthy=0 backoff_until 16min ahead; the exponential backoff (30*2^(n-1) = 60s at n=2, exactly the logged backoff_sec) is correct and the source already fails closed — the refusal is local egress (proxy/firewall), not a code defect. VERIFIED: 36/36 new LD tests green on the integrated tip (60s); regression suites test_forensics + test_live_workers_characterization 134/135 with the 1 failure (test_f3_corrupted_scaler_artifact_is_loud_not_silent) proven PRE-EXISTING on a clean origin/main 6a8aa2cb baseline worktree (identical failure there); ruff format + ruff check clean; mypy clean on all 4 touched source files; functional probes: duplicate-op-id warnings 0 across 2 app builds with an identical 421-path surface (all key endpoints present), CHECK-ACC-02 WARNING not CRITICAL on the live DB, create_app 1x over 5 sweeps. NOT fixed (out of scope, correct behaviour): REGIME TRANSITION thrash (TRENDING<->RANGING every 4-16s, prob 61-85% — correct hysteresis-free classification, would need a regime-damping design change) and ORPHAN_CLASSIFIED_UNKNOWN 3137 rows UNKNOWN_PROVENANCE recoverable=false (research data hygiene, not a log defect). | none | src/nexus_scalp/forensics/checks_accounting.py, src/nexus_scalp/web/debug_research_routes.py, src/nexus_scalp/forensics/checks_observability.py, src/nexus_scalp/application/{live_engine.py,live_workers.py}, tests/unit/test_ld{1_acc02_empty_exec,2_route_registration,3_app_build_caching,4_worker_kick_timeout}.py, log-defects-wave/CONTRACT.md, agents/taskboard.md | audit trail immutable (no DB row deleted or rewritten — LD-1 is classification only); CHECK-ACC-02 CRITICAL preserved for NEW real duplicates; /api/intelligence/* + /api/models/* route surface and ORDER unchanged (421 paths identical); worker kick still detaches a genuinely hung call; forensic check still fail-closed UNKNOWN on app-build failure; sweep cadence unchanged | none | READY_FOR_REVIEW |

New contract battery tests/unit/test_ml_qa_007_parity_latency_determinism.py (19 tests, textual analysis, runs in the slim venv with no torch import) pins all three rules: no wall-clock anchors or asserts remain, every probe is process_time, warmup precedes the first timed probe in both SLA tests with calls.clear(), the CPU mean bound and ordering invariants stay asserted, and the adapter contract is untouched (instance-level _send_request monkeypatch seam only). ## EUP-RELEASE-001 — end-user CLI help-surface identity (2026-09-24)

| TASK-ID | Owner | Priority | Title | Deps | Files | Contracts | Blocker | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| TASK-EUP-RELEASE-001 | Hermes (end-user/release lane, 2026-09-24) | P1 | End-user release audit + first remediation: the documented-identical CLI help surface diverged per entry point. Click derives the `--help` usage line from `sys.argv[0]`, so `python -m nexus_scalp.cli.main --help` printed `Usage: python -m nexus_scalp.cli.main` and the packaged `NexusScalpEngine-CLI.exe --help` printed its own filename, while the `nexus help` word form always said `nexus` — the docs/CLI.md contract ("nexus --help and nexus help are the same output") was broken on every path except the literal `nexus` console script, and error panels (which embed the usage line) followed argv[0] too. Fix: one canonical `CLI_PROGRAM_NAME` constant in release/metadata.py next to PRODUCT_DISPLAY, passed as `prog_name=` at all four first-party invocation sites (cli/main.py, cli/__main__.py, release/cli_shim.py, release/packaged_main.py); the word form already hardcoded the same name so no change there. Baseline evidence (lane worktree off origin/main 5c38c3ef, PYTHONPATH=src): tests/installer + tests/release 82 passed / 4 skipped; tests/cli had 2 failures — the help-surface one was this defect, the `--version` one was a harness artifact (the dev venv editable .pth resolved nexus_scalp to the MAIN checkout's tree, which is behind origin/main on that fix) and passes in the lane tree. New TestProgramNameInvariance (4 tests) drives the CLI through a deliberately MISNAMED wrapper (argv[0] != nexus) plus all four documented entry points and asserts they render one canonical usage line; existing tests/cli golden (help parity, --version eager exit 0, usage error exit 2 no traceback) unchanged and passing. Support matrix from evidence: windows-x64 SUPPORTED (sole packaged target), windows-arm64 UNSUPPORTED (loud arm64-report job; no torch/polars/pyarrow/MT5 wheels), Linux docker/developer-only, macOS not a packaged product. | none | src/nexus_scalp/release/metadata.py, src/nexus_scalp/cli/main.py, src/nexus_scalp/cli/__main__.py, src/nexus_scalp/release/cli_shim.py, src/nexus_scalp/release/packaged_main.py, tests/cli/test_cli_subprocess.py (harness: run_cli() now pins this tree's absolute src/ on the subprocess PYTHONPATH + entry-point trust note), agents/taskboard.md (this row), agents/locks.yaml | presentation/identity layer only — no engine, MT5, model, DB or update behavior touched (wave §80); exit-code contract (0/1/2/3/4/5) preserved; `nexus --version` stays eager exit 0; CLI subprocess tests must verdict the tree under test, never a foreign checkout reachable through the shared venv's editable install | none | DONE (PR #417 merged as squash c9851b51, verified on origin/main) |

## ML-QA-008 — push-gate latency determinism: experiment-registry benchmark (2026-09-24)
| TASK-ID | Owner | Priority | Title | Deps | Files | Contracts | Blocker | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| ML-QA-008 | AGENT-QA (NSE Autonomous ML Specialist Swarm, 2026-09-24) | P2 | Push-gate latency determinism remediation, roster candidate #6 of the ML-QA-003 census (docs/ml-system/test_determinism_roster.md section 6): tests/unit/test_experiment_registry.py carried 4 time.perf_counter() probes inside the BENCHMARK_PLAN test test_benchmark_1000_records_top10_under_50ms (critical-suite manifest line 357, runs on every push under -n auto), asserting the top-10 registry query completes in < 50ms on the WALL CLOCK — under xdist saturation on a 2-core runner that bound measures the scheduler, not the query (the same shape that forced 9279f1ea to drop the parity-suite 1ms SLA, remediated in ML-QA-007). Remediation (test-only, zero production code): (1) both benchmark legs (register-1000, top-10 query) moved to time.process_time() — CPU time, co-tenant preemption excluded by construction; (2) explicit warmup (register + finalize + top_n) runs BEFORE the first timed probe so sqlite connection/schema setup is not charged to leg 1; (3) load-bearing asserts kept hard: n=1000, every-3rd finalize stride, len(top)==10, best-element check, monotone ordering, hard query_ms < 50.0 CPU budget; (4) the only change to an existing assert is register_ms > 0.0 -> >= 0.0 — registration throughput was never gated (original comment: not gated, disk-dependent) and > 0.0 on process_time would be a NEW wall-clock-class flake when an idle leg rounds to 0.0. New contract battery tests/unit/test_ml_qa_008_registry_cpu_budget.py (6 tests, textual analysis, slim venv, no sqlite/torch import) pins: no time.perf_counter anywhere in the module, both legs start/end on process_time, warmup precedes the first timed probe and covers all three operations, scale/result/budget asserts unweakened, failure message documents the CPU clock, original test name preserved (manifest/roster references stay valid). Registered in tests/critical_suite.txt (239 paths) so it rides the required Code Quality & Tests / Pytest check — CHG-0049 gate parity honored, no CI-only gate class, no workflow file edited.
